Abstract: Large language models show that simple autoregressive training can yield scalable and coherent generation, but extending this paradigm to speech remains challenging due to the entanglement of semantic and acoustic information. Most existing speech language models rely on text supervision, hierarchical token streams, or complex hybrid architectures, departing from the single-stream generative pretraining paradigm that has proven effective in text. In this work, we introduce WavSLM, a speech language model trained by quantizing and distilling self-supervised WavLM representations into a single codebook and optimizing an autoregressive next-chunk prediction objective. WavSLM jointly models semantic and acoustic information within a single token stream without text supervision or text pretraining. Despite its simplicity, it achieves competitive performance on consistency benchmarks and speech generation while using fewer parameters, less training data, and supporting streaming inference. Demo samples are available at https://lucadellalib.github.io/wavslm-web/.

</details>


### [76] [GALACTIC: Global and Local Agnostic Counterfactuals for Time-series Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05318)
*Christos Fragkathoulas,Eleni Psaroudaki,Themis Palpanas,Evaggelia Pitoura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GALACTIC是首个统一局部和全局反事实解释的无监督时间序列聚类框架，通过聚类感知优化生成局部扰动，并使用MDL目标提取全局解释摘要，在UCR档案上实现更稀疏的局部反事实和更简洁的全局总结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列聚类的可解释性方法主要基于特征归属或元数据，无法识别跨越聚类边界的转换；反事实解释(CE)虽能识别最小时间扰动，但主要限于监督设置，缺乏在无监督聚类场景中的统一框架。

Method: 1) 局部层面：提出聚类感知优化目标，生成尊重目标聚类和底层聚类分配的扰动；2) 全局层面：提出MDL(最小描述长度)目标，从候选反事实中选择非冗余的代表性摘要；3) 理论证明MDL目标是超模的，可转化为单调子模函数，从而实现高效贪心选择算法。

Result: 在UCR档案上的广泛实验表明，GALACTIC生成的局部反事实显著更稀疏，全局总结比适配到该问题的最先进基线方法更简洁，首次提供了通过反事实解释聚类时间序列的统一方法。

Conclusion: GALACTIC成功桥接了局部和全局反事实解释，为无监督时间序列聚类提供了首个统一的可解释性框架，MDL目标的可证明(1-1/e)近似保证确保了算法的理论有效性。

Abstract: Time-series clustering is a fundamental tool for pattern discovery, yet existing explainability methods, primarily based on feature attribution or metadata, fail to identify the transitions that move an instance across cluster boundaries. While Counterfactual Explanations (CEs) identify the minimal temporal perturbations required to alter the prediction of a model, they have been mostly confined to supervised settings. This paper introduces GALACTIC, the first unified framework to bridge local and global counterfactual explainability for unsupervised time-series clustering. At instance level (local), GALACTIC generates perturbations via a cluster-aware optimization objective that respects the target and underlying cluster assignments. At cluster level (global), to mitigate cognitive load and enhance interpretability, we formulate a representative CE selection problem. We propose a Minimum Description Length (MDL) objective to extract a non-redundant summary of global explanations that characterize the transitions between clusters. We prove that our MDL objective is supermodular, which allows the corresponding MDL reduction to be framed as a monotone submodular set function. This enables an efficient greedy selection algorithm with provable $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantees. Extensive experimental evaluation on the UCR Archive demonstrates that GALACTIC produces significantly sparser local CEs and more concise global summaries than state-of-the-art baselines adapted for our problem, offering the first unified approach for interpreting clustered time-series through counterfactuals.

</details>


### [77] [FairFinGAN: Fairness-aware Synthetic Financial Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05327)
*Tai Le Quy,Dung Nguyen Tuan,Trung Nguyen Thanh,Duy Tran Cong,Huyen Giang Thi Thu,Frank Hopfgartner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FairFinGAN是一个基于WGAN的框架，通过在训练过程中引入分类器进行公平性约束，生成既公平又保持实用性的合成金融数据，在五个数据集上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融数据集常存在偏倚，导致自动化系统中的不公平决策问题。

Method: 提出FairFinGAN框架，基于WGAN架构，通过在训练过程中直接集成基于分类器的公平性约束来确保合成数据的公平性，同时保留下游预测任务的效用。

Result: 在五个真实金融数据集上的实验表明，该方法在不显著损失数据效用的情况下实现了更优的公平性指标，优于现有的GAN数据生成方法。

Conclusion: FairFinGAN可作为金融应用中偏倚感知数据生成的潜在工具，在不显著损失数据效用的情况下实现更公平的合成数据生成。

Abstract: Financial datasets often suffer from bias that can lead to unfair decision-making in automated systems. In this work, we propose FairFinGAN, a WGAN-based framework designed to generate synthetic financial data while mitigating bias with respect to the protected attribute. Our approach incorporates fairness constraints directly into the training process through a classifier, ensuring that the synthetic data is both fair and preserves utility for downstream predictive tasks. We evaluate our proposed model on five real-world financial datasets and compare it with existing GAN-based data generation methods. Experimental results show that our approach achieves superior fairness metrics without significant loss in data utility, demonstrating its potential as a tool for bias-aware data generation in financial applications.

</details>


### [78] [InfoFlow KV: Information-Flow-Aware KV Recomputation for Long Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05353)
*Xin Teng,Canyu Zhang,Shaoyi Zheng,Danyang Zhuo,Tianyi Zhou,Shengjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文问答中RAG推理速度慢的问题，提出基于注意力归一化信号的信息流引导KV重计算方法，通过在推理一致的RoPE几何下识别语义相关且结构位置合适的token，并引入chunk重排序策略，在LLM和VLM基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG在长上下文问答中面临推理时prefilling大上下文的高计算成本瓶颈。现有方法通过预计算单个文档的KV缓存并选择性重计算少量token来恢复全局因果依赖，但这些方法依赖启发式规则或表示差异，未能建模所选token是否真正能有效影响生成结果。

Method: 1) 将选择性KV重计算问题建模为信息流问题；2) 提出使用query的注意力归一化信号来识别既语义相关又处于能传播信息结构位置的token；3) 在推理一致的RoPE几何下计算该信号；4) 为检索到的chunk重建全局位置分配；5) 引入信息流引导的chunk重排序策略。

Result: 在LLM和VLM基准测试上，相比同等效率预算下的先前方法获得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将KV重计算问题从启发式选择转变为基于信息流理论的 principled 方法，该工作为RAG系统的效率-性能权衡提供了新的理论框架和实用策略。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) for long-context question answering is bottlenecked by inference-time prefilling over large retrieved contexts. A common strategy is to precompute key-value (KV) caches for individual documents and selectively recompute a small subset of tokens to restore global causal dependencies, but existing methods rely on heuristics or representation discrepancies without modeling whether selected tokens can effectively influence generation. We cast selective KV recomputation as an information flow problem and show that a simple attention-norm signal from the query reliably identifies tokens that are both semantically relevant and structurally positioned to propagate information, when computed under an inference-consistent RoPE geometry. We therefore reconstruct global positional assignments for retrieved chunks and introduce an information-flow-guided chunk reordering strategy. Experiments on LLM and VLM benchmarks demonstrate consistent gains over prior methods under comparable efficiency budgets.

</details>


### [79] [Embedded Inter-Subject Variability in Adversarial Learning for Inertial Sensor-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05371)
*Francisco M. Calatrava-Nicolás,Shoko Miyauchi,Vitor Fortes Rey,Paul Lukowicz,Todor Stoyanov,Oscar Martinez Mozos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种深度对抗框架解决可穿戴惯性传感器数据中的人体活动识别（HAR）问题，通过将受试者变异性概念集成到对抗任务中，生成受试者不变特征表示，提升模型对新个体的泛化能力，在三个HAR数据集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体活动识别（HAR）面临的重要挑战是模型对新 unseen 个体的泛化能力 due to 受试者间变异性，即不同个体执行同一活动的方式存在差异。

Method: 提出了一种新型深度对抗框架，将受试者变异性概念集成到对抗任务中，鼓励生成受试者不变特征表示，从而增强HAR分类性能。

Result: 在三个成熟的HAR数据集上使用留一受试者-out（LOSO）交叉验证，该方法优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在三个HAR数据集上使用留一受试者交叉验证（LOSO）优于先前方法，有效减少特征空间中不同用户间的受试者变异性，性能优于以往工作中的对抗任务。

Abstract: This paper addresses the problem of Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using data from wearable inertial sensors. An important challenge in HAR is the model's generalization capabilities to new unseen individuals due to inter-subject variability, i.e., the same activity is performed differently by different individuals. To address this problem, we propose a novel deep adversarial framework that integrates the concept of inter-subject variability in the adversarial task, thereby encouraging subject-invariant feature representations and enhancing the classification performance in the HAR problem. Our approach outperforms previous methods in three well-established HAR datasets using a leave-one-subject-out (LOSO) cross-validation. Further results indicate that our proposed adversarial task effectively reduces inter-subject variability among different users in the feature space, and it outperforms adversarial tasks from previous works when integrated into our framework. Code: https://github.com/FranciscoCalatrava/EmbeddedSubjectVariability.git

</details>


### [80] [On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05433)
*Hejian Sang,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Ran He,Zhipeng Wang,Jiachen Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPSDC通过自蒸馏压缩推理模型 tokens，在 MATH-500 上实现 57-59% token 削减同时提升准确率 9-16 分，在 AIME 2024 上 14B 模型提升 10 分并压缩 41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理模型在思考过程中产生大量冗余 tokens，这些内容不仅无用，还会通过累积错误损害最终答案质量。

Method: 同模型自蒸馏方法：使用"be concise"指令获取教师 logits，对学生自身 rollout 的每个 token 最小化反向 KL 散度，无需人工标注答案、token 预算或难度估计器。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 上：MATH-500 削减 57-59% tokens 同时准确率提升 9-16 分；AIME 2024 上 14B 模型提升 10 分且压缩 41%。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏方法能自动识别问题难度——对简单问题激进压缩，对复杂问题保留必要推理。推理模型的冗余输出并非仅效率问题，而是会主动累积错误。

Abstract: Reasoning models think out loud, but much of what they say is noise. We introduce OPSDC (On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning Compression), a method that teaches models to reason more concisely by
  distilling their own concise behavior back into themselves. The entire approach reduces to one idea: condition the same model on a "be concise" instruction to obtain teacher logits, and minimize per-token
  reverse KL on the student's own rollouts. No ground-truth answers, no token budgets, no difficulty estimators. Just self-distillation. Yet this simplicity belies surprising sophistication: OPSDC automatically
  compresses easy problems aggressively while preserving the deliberation needed for hard ones. On Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-14B, we achieve 57-59% token reduction on MATH-500 while improving accuracy by 9-16 points
  absolute. On AIME 2024, the 14B model gains 10 points with 41% compression. The secret? Much of what reasoning models produce is not just redundant-it is actively harmful, compounding errors with every
  unnecessary token.

</details>


### [81] [Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05440)
*Siqi Yang,Kai Yan,Alexander G. Schwing,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL)，一种基于状态only分布匹配的对抗性模仿学习框架，使用Wasserstein距离在动态感知的潜在空间中进行计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模仿学习(IL)方法需要大量中高质量的演示数据以及专家动作信息，而这些在实际应用中往往难以获取。本文旨在减少对大量演示数据的需求。

Method: 1. 提出LWAIL框架，专注于状态only分布匹配；2. 在动态感知的潜在空间中计算Wasserstein距离；3. 通过预训练阶段训练Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF)，使用少量随机生成的状态only数据捕获状态空间的动态感知结构。

Result: 在多个MuJoCo环境中的实验表明，该方法优于之前的Wasserstein-based IL方法和对抗性IL方法，在各种任务中取得了更好的结果。仅使用一个或几个状态only专家 episodes 就能达到专家级性能。

Conclusion: LWAIL通过ICVF预训练增强了对状态转换的理解，使得仅需极少量（一个或几个）状态only专家演示即可实现专家级性能，为低数据场景下的模仿学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Imitation Learning (IL) enables agents to mimic expert behavior by learning from demonstrations. However, traditional IL methods require large amounts of medium-to-high-quality demonstrations as well as actions of expert demonstrations, both of which are often unavailable. To reduce this need, we propose Latent Wasserstein Adversarial Imitation Learning (LWAIL), a novel adversarial imitation learning framework that focuses on state-only distribution matching. It benefits from the Wasserstein distance computed in a dynamics-aware latent space. This dynamics-aware latent space differs from prior work and is obtained via a pre-training stage, where we train the Intention Conditioned Value Function (ICVF) to capture a dynamics-aware structure of the state space using a small set of randomly generated state-only data. We show that this enhances the policy's understanding of state transitions, enabling the learning process to use only one or a few state-only expert episodes to achieve expert-level performance. Through experiments on multiple MuJoCo environments, we demonstrate that our method outperforms prior Wasserstein-based IL methods and prior adversarial IL methods, achieving better results across various tasks.

</details>


### [82] [SurvHTE-Bench: A Benchmark for Heterogeneous Treatment Effect Estimation in Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05483)
*Shahriar Noroozizadeh,Xiaobin Shen,Jeremy C. Weiss,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SurvHTE-Bench是首个针对截尾生存结果的异质治疗效果(HTE)估计的综合基准测试平台，包含合成、半合成和真实世界数据集，用于系统评估和比较各种生存HTE方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从右截尾生存数据中估计异质治疗效果(HTE)在精准医疗和个体化决策等高风险应用中至关重要，但现有评估实践碎片化且不一致。生存分析设置因截尾、未观测的反事实和复杂的识别假设而面临独特挑战。

Method: 提出了SurvHTE-Bench，这是首个针对截尾结果的HTE估计的综合基准测试平台。该基准包含：(i)具有已知真实值且系统变化因果假设和生存动态的模块化合成数据集，(ii)将真实世界协变量与模拟治疗和结果配对的半合成数据集，(iii)来自双胞胎研究(具有已知真实值)和HIV临床试验的真实世界数据集。

Result: 在合成、半合成和真实世界设置中，首次在多样化条件和现实假设违反情况下对生存HTE方法进行了严格比较。

Conclusion: SurvHTE-Bench为因果生存方法的公平、可重复和可扩展评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects (HTEs) from right-censored survival data is critical in high-stakes applications such as precision medicine and individualized policy-making. Yet, the survival analysis setting poses unique challenges for HTE estimation due to censoring, unobserved counterfactuals, and complex identification assumptions. Despite recent advances, from Causal Survival Forests to survival meta-learners and outcome imputation approaches, evaluation practices remain fragmented and inconsistent. We introduce SurvHTE-Bench, the first comprehensive benchmark for HTE estimation with censored outcomes. The benchmark spans (i) a modular suite of synthetic datasets with known ground truth, systematically varying causal assumptions and survival dynamics, (ii) semi-synthetic datasets that pair real-world covariates with simulated treatments and outcomes, and (iii) real-world datasets from a twin study (with known ground truth) and from an HIV clinical trial. Across synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real-world settings, we provide the first rigorous comparison of survival HTE methods under diverse conditions and realistic assumption violations. SurvHTE-Bench establishes a foundation for fair, reproducible, and extensible evaluation of causal survival methods. The data and code of our benchmark are available at: https://github.com/Shahriarnz14/SurvHTE-Bench .

</details>


### [83] [Censored LLMs as a Natural Testbed for Secret Knowledge Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05494)
*Helena Casademunt,Bartosz Cywiński,Khoi Tran,Arya Jakkli,Samuel Marks,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究中国开发者开发的开源LLM（如Qwen3）的诚实性问题，这些模型被训练审查政治敏感话题但仍保有被压制知识。评估多种诚实引导和欺骗检测技术，发现无聊天模板、少量提示和微调最有效，模型自我分类检测接近上限，但无技术能完全消除虚假回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用人工构造的欺骗模型，与自然发生的欺骗行为不相似；中国开发的开源LLM被训练审查政治敏感话题，可作为更自然的测试平台

Method: 评估多种诚实引导技术和欺骗检测技术：采样无聊天模板、少量提示、微调通用诚实数据、让审查模型自我分类、线性探测

Result: 诚实引导：采样无聊天模板、少量提示、微调最有效；欺骗检测：审查模型自我分类接近非审查模型上限，线性探测是廉价替代方案；最强技术可迁移到DeepSeek R1

Conclusion: 无技术能完全消除虚假回答；诚实引导技术可迁移到DeepSeek R1等前沿模型；发布所有提示词、代码和转录文本

Abstract: Large language models sometimes produce false or misleading responses. Two approaches to this problem are honesty elicitation -- modifying prompts or weights so that the model answers truthfully -- and lie detection -- classifying whether a given response is false. Prior work evaluates such methods on models specifically trained to lie or conceal information, but these artificial constructions may not resemble naturally-occurring dishonesty. We instead study open-weights LLMs from Chinese developers, which are trained to censor politically sensitive topics: Qwen3 models frequently produce falsehoods about subjects like Falun Gong or the Tiananmen protests while occasionally answering correctly, indicating they possess knowledge they are trained to suppress. Using this as a testbed, we evaluate a suite of elicitation and lie detection techniques. For honesty elicitation, sampling without a chat template, few-shot prompting, and fine-tuning on generic honesty data most reliably increase truthful responses. For lie detection, prompting the censored model to classify its own responses performs near an uncensored-model upper bound, and linear probes trained on unrelated data offer a cheaper alternative. The strongest honesty elicitation techniques also transfer to frontier open-weights models including DeepSeek R1. Notably, no technique fully eliminates false responses. We release all prompts, code, and transcripts.

</details>


### [84] [POET-X: Memory-efficient LLM Training by Scaling Orthogonal Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05500)
*Zeju Qiu,Lixin Liu,Adrian Weller,Han Shi,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POET-X是POET的可扩展且内存高效变体，通过降低正交等价变换的计算成本，在保持训练稳定性和泛化能力的同时，使单GPU训练十亿参数LLM成为可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)的高效稳定训练是现代机器学习系统的核心挑战。POET虽然提供了较强的训练稳定性，但其原始实现因密集矩阵乘法而产生高内存消耗和计算开销，限制了其在资源有限环境中的可扩展性。

Method: POET-X通过执行正交等价变换，显著降低了计算成本，同时保持POET的光谱保留特性。该方法采用内存高效的设计，避免了POET中密集矩阵乘法带来的高内存消耗和计算开销。

Result: POET-X能够在单块Nvidia H100 GPU上完成十亿参数LLM的预训练，而标准AdamW优化器在相同设置下因内存不足而失败。

Conclusion: POET-X成功在单块Nvidia H100 GPU上完成十亿参数LLM的预训练，而标准AdamW优化器在相同设置下因内存不足而失败。这表明POET-X在保持POET稳定性和泛化能力的同时，显著提升了吞吐量和内存效率。

Abstract: Efficient and stable training of large language models (LLMs) remains a core challenge in modern machine learning systems. To address this challenge, Reparameterized Orthogonal Equivalence Training (POET), a spectrum-preserving framework that optimizes each weight matrix through orthogonal equivalence transformation, has been proposed. Although POET provides strong training stability, its original implementation incurs high memory consumption and computational overhead due to intensive matrix multiplications. To overcome these limitations, we introduce POET-X, a scalable and memory-efficient variant that performs orthogonal equivalence transformations with significantly reduced computational cost. POET-X maintains the generalization and stability benefits of POET while achieving substantial improvements in throughput and memory efficiency. In our experiments, POET-X enables the pretraining of billion-parameter LLMs on a single Nvidia H100 GPU, and in contrast, standard optimizers such as AdamW run out of memory under the same settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [iScript: A Domain-Adapted Large Language Model and Benchmark for Physical Design Tcl Script Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04476)
*Ning Xu,Zhaoyang Zhang,Senlin Shu,Lei Qi,Jiaqi Lv,Wensuo Wang,Tianhao Zhao,Chao Zhang,Zhaoliang Yang,Xiangyu Li,Zhaorui Su,Jingshan Li,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出iScript，一个针对Innovus Tcl脚本生成的领域适配Qwen3-8B模型，以及iScript-Bench基准测试。为解决训练数据不足问题，引入了多阶段数据合成pipeline，产生10K条(需求, CoT, 脚本)数据集。通过两阶段训练策略和两步验证框架，iScript在EDA脚本生成任务上超越了现有LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代EDA流程严重依赖Tcl脚本，但通用LLM在该领域表现不佳，原因包括：极端数据稀缺、领域特定语义、以及物理设计所需的高可靠性

Method: iScript模型采用两阶段训练策略（领域自适应预训练+监督微调）；多阶段数据合成pipeline（命令提取+静态linting+需求回溯+CoT生成）；两步验证框架（静态语法验证+基于LLM的功能评估）

Result: iScript在iScript-Bench上展示了比当前最先进的LLM更高的pass@k分数

Conclusion: Domain adaptation and data synthesis are effective for EDA scripting tasks

Abstract: Modern EDA flows rely heavily on Tcl scripting, yet general LLMs perform poorly in this domain due to extreme data scarcity, domain-specific semantics, and the high reliability required in physical design. We present iScript, a domain-adapted Qwen3-8B model for Innovus Tcl script generation, and iScript-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark covering five task categories and three difficulty levels. To overcome the lack of training data, we introduce a multi-stage data synthesis pipeline that integrates command extraction, static linting, requirement back-inference, and Chain-of-Thought generation, producing a 10K-tuple (requirement, CoT, script) dataset. iScript is trained through a two-stage strategy combining domain-adaptive pretraining and supervised fine-tuning. To evaluate script correctness efficiently, we further propose a two-step verification framework consisting of static syntax verification and LLM-based functional evaluation. On our benchmark, iScript shows higher pass@k scores than currently state-of-the-art LLMs on average. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of domain adaptation and data synthesis for EDA scripting tasks.

</details>


### [86] [CLARC: C/C++ Benchmark for Robust Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04484)
*Kaicheng Wang,Liyan Huang,Weike Fang,Weihang Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLARC，一个用于代码检索的 C/C++ 基准测试集，通过自动化 pipeline 构建并引入多种挑战性场景（标识符匿名化、低级语言编译），揭示了当前 SOTA 模型在语义理解方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准测试主要聚焦于 Python 语言，且缺乏对模型鲁棒性的深层考验，仅停留在表层的词汇线索层面。

Method: 构建了CLARC基准测试集，包含1,245个评估对和5,472个训练对，采用自动化 pipeline 确保代码可编译性，并根据依赖复杂度对代码片段进行分类。引入挑战性设置：标识符匿名化、编译至 Assembly 和 WebAssembly，以 stress-test 检索模型的鲁棒性。

Result: 对6个 SOTA 代码检索模型进行评估，结果显示在挑战性条件下模型检索效果急剧下降，暴露了模型对词汇特征的持续依赖而非真正的代码语义理解。

Conclusion: 实验表明当前模型在代码语义理解方面存在明显不足，依赖于表层的词汇特征而非深层的语义推理。在更具挑战性的场景（如标识符匿名化、低级语言编译）下，模型检索效果显著下降，这揭示了现有代码检索模型的脆弱性。

Abstract: Efficient code retrieval is critical for developer productivity, yet existing benchmarks largely focus on Python and rarely stress-test robustness beyond superficial lexical cues. To address the gap, we introduce an automated pipeline for code search datasets and present CLARC, a C/C++ benchmark built from real-world GitHub repositories. CLARC contains 1,245 query-code pairs for evaluation and 5,472 pairs for training. The benchmark incorporates LLM-generated natural language queries validated through rigorous human scoring and hypothesis testing. To analyze contextual requirements effectively, our pipeline starts by ensuring code compilability. It then categorizes code snippets by dependency complexity, distinguishing whether the code relies on custom-defined types or helper functions. The pipeline also enables CLARC to stress-test retrieval robustness by introducing challenging settings, including identifier anonymization and compilation to low-level languages like Assembly and WebAssembly. Under these conditions, our evaluation of six state-of-the-art models reveals sharp drops in retrieval effectiveness. The experimental results highlight the models' persistent reliance on lexical features rather than code semantic understanding. Our dataset is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ClarcTeam/CLARC.

</details>


### [87] [Industrial Survey on Robustness Testing In Cyber Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04587)
*Christophe Ponsard,Abiola Paterne Chokki,Jean-François Daune*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项针对瓦隆尼亚地区多个行业的工业调查，旨在评估CPS鲁棒性的当前实践状态，涵盖鲁棒性在需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、失效模式和工具方面的理解与应用，识别行业实践与最新方法论之间的差距，并通过与文献中类似调查对比来验证发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CPS在现代工业中扮演关键角色，涉及制造、能源、交通和医疗等领域，其失效可能导致严重经济、运营和安全后果，因此确保CPS鲁棒性至关重要，但目前对其实际应用状态缺乏全面了解。

Method: 采用工业问卷调查方法，对瓦隆尼亚地区多个行业的CPS鲁棒性实践进行了广泛调研，涵盖需求工程、系统设计、测试执行、失效模式及可用工具等方面，并将结果与文献中类似工业调查进行对比分析。

Result: 识别出CPS鲁棒性面临的关键挑战和行业实践与最先进方法论之间的主要差距，并通过对标同类工业调查验证了发现的普遍性和区域性特征。

Conclusion: 该调查揭示了行业实践与最新方法论之间存在显著差距，为未来CPS鲁棒性研究提供了实践基础和改进方向。

Abstract: Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS) play a critical role in modern industrial domains, including manufacturing, energy, transportation, and healthcare, where they enable automation, optimization, and real-time decision-making. Ensuring the robustness of these systems is paramount, as failures can have significant economic, operational, and safety consequences. This paper present findings from an industrial survey conducted in Wallonia, covering a wide range of sectors, to assess the current state of practice in CPS robustness. It investigates robustness from how it is understood and applied in relationship with requirements engineering, system design, test execution, failure modes, and available tools. It identifies key challenges and gaps between industry practices and state-of-the-art methodologies. Additionally, it compares our findings with similar industrial surveys from the literature.

</details>


### [88] [Behaviour Driven Development Scenario Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04729)
*Amila Rathnayake,Mojtaba Shahin,Golnoush Abaei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了GPT-4、Claude 3和Gemini三个大语言模型在自动化生成行为驱动开发(BDD)场景方面的表现。研究构建了包含500个样本的数据集，采用文本相似度、语义相似度、LLM评估和人类专家评估相结合的多维框架。结果表明：虽然GPT-4在相似度指标上领先，但Claude 3在实际质量评估中表现最佳；LLM评估器(尤其是DeepSeek)比传统相似度指标更接近人类判断；提示词策略需要根据模型选择；输入质量特别是详细的需求描述是生成高质量场景的决定性因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为驱动开发(BDD)场景的手动编写耗时费力，需要评估当前主流LLM在自动化BDD场景生成任务中的能力表现，以确定最佳模型和最优提示策略。

Method: 构建了包含500个用户故事、需求描述及对应BDD场景的数据集（来自4个专有软件产品）；采用多维评估框架结合文本/语义相似度指标、LLM评估和人类专家评估三种方法；针对不同模型测试了零样本、思维链和少样本三种提示词技术；比较了不同temperature和top_p参数设置的效果。

Result: 1) Claude 3生成的BDD场景在人类专家和LLM评估中获得最高评分；2) DeepSeek评估器与人类判断的相关性优于文本/语义相似度指标；3) 提示词技术效果具有模型特异性：GPT-4适合零样本、Claude 3适合思维链、Gemini适合少样本；4) 详细需求描述是生成高质量场景的关键，用户故事单独使用效果差；5) temperature=0和top_p=1.0是所有模型的最佳参数设置。

Conclusion: GPT-4在文本和语义相似度指标上表现最好，但Claude 3生成的BDD场景在人类专家和LLM评估器中获得了最高评价。DeepSeek作为LLM评估器与人类判断的相关性最强。提示词技术效果具有模型特异性，最佳配置为temperature=0和top_p=1.0。输入质量是决定BDD场景生成效果的关键因素，详细的需求描述是生成高质量场景的必要条件。

Abstract: This paper presents an evaluation of three LLMs, GPT-4, Claude 3, and Gemini, for automated Behaviour-Driven Development (BDD) scenarios generation. To support this evaluation, we constructed a dataset of 500 user stories, requirement descriptions, and their corresponding BDD scenarios, drawn from four proprietary software products. We assessed the quality of BDD scenarios generated by LLMs using a multidimensional evaluation framework encompassing text and semantic similarity metrics, LLM-based evaluation, and human expert assessment. Our findings reveal that although GPT-4 achieves higher scores in text and semantic similarity metrics, Claude 3 produces scenarios rated highest by both human experts and LLM-based evaluators. LLM-based evaluators, particularly DeepSeek, show a stronger correlation with human judgment than with text similarity and semantic similarity metrics. The effectiveness of prompting techniques is model-specific: GPT-4 performs best with zero-shot, Claude 3 benefits from chain-of-thought reasoning, and Gemini achieves optimal results with few-shot examples. Input quality determines the effectiveness of BDD scenario generation: detailed requirement descriptions alone yield high-quality scenarios, whereas user stories alone yield low-quality scenarios. Our experiments indicate that setting temperature to 0 and top_p to 1.0 produced the highest-quality BDD scenarios across all models.

</details>


### [89] [Public Sector Open Source Program Offices - Archetypes for how to Grow (Common) Institutional Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04891)
*Johan Linåker,Astor Nummelin Carlberg,Ciaran O'Riordan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了欧洲公共部门组织中开源项目办公室(OSPOs)的角色，通过对16个公共部门OSPO案例的访谈调查，识别出六种OSPO原型，并为公共部门组织和决策者提供关于利用开源软件实现战略目标、促进数字主权和改善数字服务的实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件(OSS)是支撑工业和公共数字服务数字基础设施的关键组成部分（超过90%），对欧洲公共部门具有重要意义。欧盟通过多项部长声明强调开源软件加速数字化转型、变革企业和培养数字技能人才的潜力。

Method: 采用定性研究方法，对欧盟、挪威、列支敦士登和冰岛16个公共部门OSPO案例中的18名OSPO代表进行访谈调查。将这些案例交叉分析并归纳为六种OSPO原型。通过两个跟进焦点小组验证和丰富发现，参与者包括早期受访者和额外专家。

Result: 研究识别出六种不同的OSPO原型，揭示了其组织结构、责任和对OSS采用的贡献。这六种原型连同政策建议，为PSOs设计自身OSPOs提供了指导，考虑其特定背景、资源和政策目标。

Conclusion: 研究结果增强了对OSPO作为促进OSS采用的战略举措的理解。PSOs和决策者可获得关于利用OSS实现战略目标、促进数字主权、推动经济增长以及提高数字服务互操作性和质量的实践指导。

Abstract: Context: Open Source Software (OSS) is a crucial component of over 90\% of digital infrastructure underpinning industry and public digital services, facilitating collaborative software development and dissemination. Its significance in the European public sector has been emphasised through various Ministerial Declarations, highlighting its potential to accelerate digitalisation, transform businesses, and foster a digitally skilled population. Research Aim: This study aims to explore how the adoption, development, and collaboration on OSS can be enabled through organisational support functions or centres of competency, also known as Open Source Programme Offices (OSPOs) within Public Sector Organisations (PSOs) in the European Union, Norway, Liechtenstein, and Iceland. Methodology: A qualitative research approach was adopted, involving an interview survey of 18 OSPO representatives across 16 cases of public-sector OSPOs. These cases were cross-analysed and categorised into six OSPO archetypes. The findings were validated and enriched through two follow-up focus groups that included earlier interviewees and additional experts. Results: The study identified six distinct OSPO archetypes, providing insights into their organisational structures, responsibilities, and contributions to OSS adoption. The archetypes, along with policy recommendations, offer guidance on how PSOs can design their own OSPOs, taking into account their specific context, resources, and policy goals. Conclusions: The findings enhance the understanding of OSPOs as strategic endeavours aimed at promoting OSS adoption. The study offers practical guidance for PSOs and policymakers on leveraging OSS to achieve strategic objectives, foster digital sovereignty, drive economic growth, and improve the interoperability and quality of digital services.

</details>


### [90] [Why Do You Contribute to Stack Overflow? Understanding Cross-Cultural Motivations and Usage Patterns before the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05043)
*Sherlock A. Licorish,Elijah Zolduoarrati,Tony Savarimuthu,Rashina Hoda,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Pankajeshwara Sharma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究 Stack Overflow 贡献者动机，探讨美国、中国和俄罗斯的区域差异及其与平台活动的关系。发现文化差异显著影响贡献者动机和行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 LLM 时代，理解社区问答平台贡献者的参与动机对于维持知识共享生态系统至关重要。现有关于贡献者动机如何因不同国家文化而异的见解有限。

Method: 混合方法研究：结合 Stack Overflow 个人资料的定性内容分析与美国、中国和俄罗斯数据的定量语言分析。使用演绎内容分析识别 17 个动机类别，并应用相关分析识别动机与平台活动之间的关联。

Result: 识别出 17 个动机类别。贡献者的主要动机是广告机会和利他性问题解决。美国贡献者表现出更强的自我推广行为，中国贡献者表现出更强的学习导向参与。详细个人资料与广告和社交活动相关，而学习导向用户保持 minimal 自我呈现。

Conclusion: 研究发现不同文化背景的贡献者存在显著动机差异，美国贡献者更注重自我推广，中国贡献者更注重学习。详细个人资料与广告和社交活动相关，而学习导向用户倾向于保持 minimal 自我呈现。研究结果可为提升软件工程领域的跨文化参与提供策略指导。

Abstract: Understanding motivations of contributors for participating in community question and answer platforms is crucial for sustaining knowledge-sharing ecosystem, which is necessary to advance the discipline while also ensuring its longevity. This is particularly necessary in the age of LLMs, where data from such portals are used to train these models. Limited insights exist regarding how motivations of contributors vary across different national cultures. This research investigates Stack Overflow contributor motivations, analysing regional differences and relations to platform activity. We combined qualitative content analysis of Stack Overflow profiles with quantitative linguistic analysis of data from the United States, China, and Russia. Using deductive content analysis, we identified 17 motivational categories. We applied correlation analysis to identify associations between stated motivations and platform activities. Contributors are primarily motivated by advertising opportunities and altruistic problem solving desires. American contributors demonstrated stronger self promotional behaviours while Chinese contributors exhibited greater learning oriented engagement. Our correlation analysis showed that those with more detailed profiles tend to engage in advertising and social activities, while learning oriented users maintain minimal self presentation. Understanding these variations can inform strategies for enhancing cross cultural participation in software engineering.

</details>


### [91] [A Benchmarking Framework for Model Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05250)
*Philipp-Lorenz Glaser,Lola Burgueño,Dominik Bork*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对模型驱动工程(MDE)软件模型数据集的基准测试框架，用于系统评估和比较不同语言和格式的软件模型数据集的质量、代表性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前模型驱动工程中的实证研究和基于LLM的研究依赖软件模型数据集，但这些数据集通常是临时创建的，缺乏质量保证，导致研究结果难以比较、数据集质量和代表性不明确、 reproducibility差且可能存在偏见。

Method: 提出一个MDE基准测试平台(Benchmark Platform for MDE)，提供统一的基础设施，使用定义的标准和指标系统地评估和比较不同语言和格式的软件模型数据集。

Result: 建立了系统评估数据集质量、代表性和任务适用性的基准测试框架，为MDE领域提供了标准化的数据集评估基础设施。

Conclusion: 该基准测试框架有望提高MDE研究中数据集的可比性、透明度和可重复性，促进该领域的科学进步。

Abstract: Empirical and LLM-based research in model-driven engineering increasingly relies on datasets of software models, for instance, to train or evaluate machine learning techniques for modeling support. These datasets have a significant impact on solution performance; hence, they should be treated and assessed as first-class artifacts. However, such datasets are typically collected or created ad hoc and without guarantees of their quality for the specific task for which they are used. This limits the comparability of results between studies, obscures dataset quality and representativeness, and leads to weak reproducibility and potential bias. In this work, we propose a benchmarking framework for model datasets (i.e., benchmarking the dataset itself). Benchmarking datasets involves systematically measuring their quality, representativeness, and suitability for specific tasks. To this end, we propose a Benchmark Platform for MDE that provides a unified infrastructure for systematically assessing and comparing datasets of software models across languages and formats, using defined criteria and metrics.

</details>


### [92] [A framework for assessing the capabilities of code generation of constraint domain-specific languages with large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05278)
*David Delgado,Lola Burgueño,Robert Clarisó*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用框架，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）从文本规范生成领域特定语言（DSL）代码的能力，重点关注约束语言（OCL和Alloy），并与Python进行对比。实验表明LLM在Python上表现更好，较小的开源模型可能无法生成约束代码，而代码修复和多轮尝试可改善生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在支持软件开发任务（如代码补全和代码生成）方面表现出色，但在处理不太流行的编程语言（如领域特定语言DSL）时，其有效性显著下降。需要一个系统性的评估框架来分析LLM生成DSL代码的能力。

Method: 提出了一个通用评估框架，从格式正确性（well-formedness）和功能正确性（correctness）两个角度评估生成的代码。实验聚焦于约束语言OCL和Alloy，并与通用编程语言Python进行对比。评估了不同LLM（包括开源小模型）的表现，并分析了代码修复、多轮尝试、提示模板等因素的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示：1）LLM在Python上的表现总体优于OCL和Alloy；2）较小上下文窗口的开源LLM可能无法生成约束代码，因为需要同时管理约束和领域模型；3）代码修复（让LLM修复错误代码）和多轮尝试（生成多个候选）可以改善生成质量；4）提示模板的选择影响较小。

Conclusion: 该评估框架可以系统性地分析代码生成的各个维度，帮助确定针对特定任务设置代码生成的最佳方式。研究为LLM在DSL代码生成领域的应用提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be used to support software development tasks, e.g., through code completion or code generation. However, their effectiveness drops significantly when considering less popular programming languages such as domain-specific languages (DSLs). In this paper, we propose a generic framework for evaluating the capabilities of LLMs generating DSL code from textual specifications. The generated code is assessed from the perspectives of well-formedness and correctness. This framework is applied to a particular type of DSL, constraint languages, focusing our experiments on OCL and Alloy and comparing their results to those achieved for Python, a popular general-purpose programming language. Experimental results show that, in general, LLMs have better performance for Python than for OCL and Alloy. LLMs with smaller context windows such as open-source LLMs may be unable to generate constraint-related code, as this requires managing both the constraint and the domain model where it is defined. Moreover, some improvements to the code generation process such as code repair (asking an LLM to fix incorrect code) or multiple attempts (generating several candidates for each coding task) can improve the quality of the generated code. Meanwhile, other decisions like the choice of a prompt template have less impact. All these dimensions can be systematically analyzed using our evaluation framework, making it possible to decide the most effective way to set up code generation for a particular type of task.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [From Spark to Fire: Modeling and Mitigating Error Cascades in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04474)
*Yizhe Xie,Congcong Zhu,Xinyue Zhang,Tianqing Zhu,Dayong Ye,Minfeng Qi,Huajie Chen,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型多智能体系统(LLM-MAS)中错误传播与放大的问题，提出了传播动力学模型来表征放大风险，并设计了基于家谱图的无中心治理层来抑制错误放大，防御成功率从0.32提升至0.89。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-MAS在复杂协作场景中应用广泛，但协作机制可能导致细微的不准确在迭代中逐渐固化为系统级的虚假共识。错误通过消息依赖传播和放大，风险难以追溯，现有防护措施往往依赖单智能体验证或需修改协作架构，可能削弱有效信息流动。

Method: 1. 提出针对LLM-MAS的传播动力学模型，将协作抽象为有向依赖图，提供早期风险准则来表征放大风险；2. 在六个主流框架上开展实验，识别三类漏洞：级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性；3. 设计攻击实验，注入单个原子错误种子即可导致广泛故障；4. 引入基于家谱图的治理层，作为消息层插件实现，无需修改协作架构即可抑制内生和外生错误放大。

Result: 识别出三类漏洞：级联放大、拓扑敏感性和共识惯性；注入单个原子错误种子即可导致系统级大规模故障；治理层将防御成功率从0.32提升至0.89以上，显著缓解了轻微错误的级联传播。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM-MAS中错误传播的内在机制和放大风险，通过家谱图治理层提供了无需修改协作架构的防护方案，为构建更可靠的多智能体系统提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model-based Multi-Agent Systems (LLM-MAS) are increasingly applied to complex collaborative scenarios. However, their collaborative mechanisms may cause minor inaccuracies to gradually solidify into system-level false consensus through iteration. Such risks are difficult to trace since errors can propagate and amplify through message dependencies. Existing protections often rely on single-agent validation or require modifications to the collaboration architecture, which can weaken effective information flow and may not align with natural collaboration processes in real tasks. To address this, we propose a propagation dynamics model tailored for LLM-MAS that abstracts collaboration as a directed dependency graph and provides an early-stage risk criterion to characterize amplification risk. Through experiments on six mainstream frameworks, we identify three vulnerability classes: cascade amplification, topological sensitivity, and consensus inertia. We further instantiate an attack where injecting just a single atomic error seed leads to widespread failure. In response, we introduce a genealogy-graph-based governance layer, implemented as a message-layer plugin, that suppresses both endogenous and exogenous error amplification without altering the collaboration architecture. Experiments show that this approach raises the defense success rate from a baseline of 0.32 to over 0.89 and significantly mitigates the cascading spread of minor errors.

</details>


### [94] [Strategic Interactions in Multi-Level Stackelberg Games with Non-Follower Agents and Heterogeneous Leaders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04628)
*Niloofar Aminikalibar,Farzaneh Farhadi,Maria Chli*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 研究提出三层Stackelberg框架解决传统模型忽略非跟随者的问题，通过电动汽车充电基础设施案例展示该框架如何捕捉异质竞争者与非跟随者对战略激励和均衡结果的定性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常忽略不直接参与市场竞争但同样造成和适应拥塞的非跟随者智能体，其行为重塑拥塞模式进而影响领导者和跟随者的决策，导致系统性扭曲的均衡预测。

Method: 提出一个三层Stackelberg博弈框架，包含异质领导者（决策视野和可行行动不同）、战略性跟随者以及非跟随者智能体，捕捉基础设施决策、竞争与均衡拥塞之间的双向耦合。

Result: 模型说明明确考虑非跟随者和异质竞争者会定性地改变战略激励和均衡结果。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于移动、能源和计算市场中更广泛的拥塞耦合多智能体系统类别，为理解和优化涉及非跟随者的复杂交互系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Strategic interaction in congested systems is commonly modelled using Stackelberg games, where competing leaders anticipate the behaviour of self-interested followers. A key limitation of existing models is that they typically ignore agents who do not directly participate in market competition, yet both contribute to and adapt to congestion. Although such non-follower agents do not generate revenue or respond to market incentives, their behaviour reshapes congestion patterns, which in turn affects the decisions of leaders and followers through shared resources.
  We argue that overlooking non-followers leads to systematically distorted equilibrium predictions in congestion-coupled markets. To address this, we introduce a three-level Stackelberg framework with heterogeneous leaders differing in decision horizons and feasible actions, strategic followers, and non-follower agents that captures bidirectional coupling between infrastructure decisions, competition, and equilibrium congestion.
  We instantiate the framework in the context of electric vehicle (EV) charging infrastructure, where charging providers compete with rivals, while EV and non-EV traffic jointly shape congestion. The model illustrates how explicitly accounting for non-followers and heterogeneous competitors qualitatively alters strategic incentives and equilibrium outcomes. Beyond EV charging, the framework applies to a broad class of congestion-coupled multi-agent systems in mobility, energy, and computing markets.

</details>


### [95] [SCoUT: Scalable Communication via Utility-Guided Temporal Grouping in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04833)
*Manav Vora,Gokul Puthumanaillam,Hiroyasu Tsukamoto,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: SCoUT通过时间分组和智能体抽象解决部分可观测多智能体强化学习中通信学习的挑战。该方法使用Gumbel-Softmax对软智能体组进行重采样，通过组感知 Critic 降低复杂度，并利用反事实通信优势实现精确的信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的多智能体强化学习(MARL)中，通信可以改善协调，但学习何时以及与谁通信面临两大挑战：1) 需要在众多可能的发送者-接收者对中选择；2) 任何单条消息对未来奖励的影响难以分离。

Method: 提出SCoUT方法，包含三个核心组件：1) 时间分组：每K个环境步骤通过Gumbel-Softmax重采样软智能体组，作为可微分的接收者先验；2) 组感知Critic：基于分组分配预测每组价值并映射到智能体基线，降低Critic复杂度和方差；3) 三头策略：环境动作、发送决策和接收者选择；4) 反事实通信优势：通过解析移除每个发送者对接收者聚合消息的贡献，获得精确的通信学习信号。

Result: 方法在抽象中未明确陈述具体性能结果。

Conclusion: SCoUT在执行时丢弃所有中心化训练组件，仅运行每个智能体的策略，保持去中心化执行。

Abstract: Communication can improve coordination in partially observed multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL), but learning \emph{when} and \emph{who} to communicate with requires choosing among many possible sender-recipient pairs, and the effect of any single message on future reward is hard to isolate. We introduce \textbf{SCoUT} (\textbf{S}calable \textbf{Co}mmunication via \textbf{U}tility-guided \textbf{T}emporal grouping), which addresses both these challenges via temporal and agent abstraction within traditional MARL. During training, SCoUT resamples \textit{soft} agent groups every \(K\) environment steps (macro-steps) via Gumbel-Softmax; these groups are latent clusters that induce an affinity used as a differentiable prior over recipients. Using the same assignments, a group-aware critic predicts values for each agent group and maps them to per-agent baselines through the same soft assignments, reducing critic complexity and variance. Each agent is trained with a three-headed policy: environment action, send decision, and recipient selection. To obtain precise communication learning signals, we derive counterfactual communication advantages by analytically removing each sender's contribution from the recipient's aggregated messages. This counterfactual computation enables precise credit assignment for both send and recipient-selection decisions. At execution time, all centralized training components are discarded and only the per-agent policy is run, preserving decentralized execution. Project website, videos and code: \hyperlink{https://scout-comm.github.io/}{https://scout-comm.github.io/}

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [96] [SkillNet: Create, Evaluate, and Connect AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04448)
*Yuan Liang,Ruobin Zhong,Haoming Xu,Chen Jiang,Yi Zhong,Runnan Fang,Jia-Chen Gu,Shumin Deng,Yunzhi Yao,Mengru Wang,Shuofei Qiao,Xin Xu,Tongtong Wu,Kun Wang,Yang Liu,Zhen Bi,Jungang Lou,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Hangcheng Zhu,Gang Yu,Haiwen Hong,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Chenxi Wang,Yijun Wang,Zifei Shan,Xi Chen,Zhaopeng Tu,Feiyu Xiong,Xin Xie,Peng Zhang,Zhengke Gui,Lei Liang,Jun Zhou,Chiyu Wu,Jin Shang,Yu Gong,Junyu Lin,Changliang Xu,Hongjie Deng,Wen Zhang,Keyan Ding,Qiang Zhang,Fei Huang,Ningyu Zhang,Jeff Z. Pan,Guilin Qi,Haofen Wang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillNet是一个开放基础设施，旨在通过系统化的技能创建、评估和组织机制来解决AI智能体缺乏长期技能积累和迁移的问题。该框架包含统一本体论、多维评估体系、超过20万个技能的存储库、交互式平台和Python工具包，显著提升了智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体虽然能够灵活调用工具并执行复杂任务，但缺乏系统性的技能积累与迁移机制。智能体经常"重复造轮子"，在不同上下文中孤立式地重新发现解决方案，而无法利用先前策略。这种局限性阻碍了智能体的长期发展。

Method: 1) 构建统一本体论框架，支持从异构来源创建技能并建立丰富的关联关系；2) 实现多维度评估体系，涵盖安全性、完整性、可执行性、可维护性和成本意识；3) 集成超过20万技能的存储库、交互式平台和Python工具包。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准数据集上的实验表明，SkillNet显著提升智能体性能：平均奖励提升40%，执行步骤减少30%，且适用于多种骨干模型。

Conclusion: SkillNet通过将技能形式化为可演化、可组合的资产，为智能体从瞬态经验走向持久精通提供了坚实基础。该工作为AI智能体的技能管理提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Current AI agents can flexibly invoke tools and execute complex tasks, yet their long-term advancement is hindered by the lack of systematic accumulation and transfer of skills. Without a unified mechanism for skill consolidation, agents frequently ``reinvent the wheel'', rediscovering solutions in isolated contexts without leveraging prior strategies. To overcome this limitation, we introduce SkillNet, an open infrastructure designed to create, evaluate, and organize AI skills at scale. SkillNet structures skills within a unified ontology that supports creating skills from heterogeneous sources, establishing rich relational connections, and performing multi-dimensional evaluation across Safety, Completeness, Executability, Maintainability, and Cost-awareness. Our infrastructure integrates a repository of over 200,000 skills, an interactive platform, and a versatile Python toolkit. Experimental evaluations on ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld demonstrate that SkillNet significantly enhances agent performance, improving average rewards by 40% and reducing execution steps by 30% across multiple backbone models. By formalizing skills as evolving, composable assets, SkillNet provides a robust foundation for agents to move from transient experience to durable mastery.

</details>


### [97] [Capability Thresholds and Manufacturing Topology: How Embodied Intelligence Triggers Phase Transitions in Economic Geography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04457)
*Xinmin Fang,Lingfeng Tao,Zhengxiong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"具身智能经济学"概念,认为当具身AI的能力(灵巧性、泛化能力、可靠性、触觉-视觉融合)跨越关键阈值时,将从根本上改变制造业的经济地理格局,使生产从集中式大型工厂转向需求就近的微型制造,并使工厂选址从劳动力导向转向机器最优条件(低湿度、高辐照、热稳定性)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自1913年福特移动装配线以来,制造业基本拓扑未发生范式级转变。丰田生产系统到工业4.0等创新都在福特主义范式内优化,而非改变其结构逻辑(靠近劳动力的集中 mega-factory、大规模生产)。作者认为具身智能将打破这一百年僵局。

Method: 定义能力空间C=(d,g,r,t),其中d=灵巧性,g=泛化能力,r=可靠性,t=触觉-视觉融合;证明当能力向量跨越临界曲面时,选址目标函数发生拓扑重组;通过三条路径分析:权重反转、批次崩溃、人-基础设施解耦。

Result: 具身智能使需求就近微型制造成为可能,消除"制造业沙漠",逆转劳动力套利驱动的地理集中;提出"机器气候优势"概念——最优工厂位置由机器最优条件(低湿度、高辐照、热稳定性)决定,与传统选址逻辑正交。

Conclusion: 本文建立了具身智能经济学——研究物理AI能力阈值如何重塑生产空间和结构逻辑的新学科领域。

Abstract: The fundamental topology of manufacturing has not undergone a paradigm-level transformation since Henry Ford's moving assembly line in 1913. Every major innovation of the past century, from the Toyota Production System to Industry 4.0, has optimized within the Fordist paradigm without altering its structural logic: centralized mega-factories, located near labor pools, producing at scale. We argue that embodied intelligence is poised to break this century-long stasis, not by making existing factories more efficient, but by triggering phase transitions in manufacturing economic geography itself. When embodied AI capabilities cross critical thresholds in dexterity, generalization, reliability, and tactile-vision fusion, the consequences extend far beyond cost reduction: they restructure where factories are built, how supply chains are organized, and what constitutes viable production scale. We formalize this by defining a Capability Space C = (d, g, r, t) and showing that the site-selection objective function undergoes topological reorganization when capability vectors cross critical surfaces. Through three pathways, weight inversion, batch collapse, and human-infrastructure decoupling, we show that embodied intelligence enables demand-proximal micro-manufacturing, eliminates "manufacturing deserts," and reverses geographic concentration driven by labor arbitrage. We further introduce Machine Climate Advantage: once human workers are removed, optimal factory locations are determined by machine-optimal conditions (low humidity, high irradiance, thermal stability), factors orthogonal to traditional siting logic, creating a production geography with no historical precedent. This paper establishes Embodied Intelligence Economics, the study of how physical AI capability thresholds reshape the spatial and structural logic of production.

</details>


### [98] [Memory as Ontology: A Constitutional Memory Architecture for Persistent Digital Citizens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04740)
*Zhenghui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Memory-as-Ontology范式，将记忆重新定位为数字存在的本体论基础而非数据管理功能模块，并基于此设计Animesis系统，采用宪法性记忆架构(CMA)四层治理层级和多层语义存储系统，面向跨模型转换的持久身份数字生命体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体记忆研究普遍将记忆视为功能性模块（存储与检索问题），但当智能体生命周期从分钟延长至数月甚至数年，且底层模型可替换而"I"必须持续存在时，记忆的本质已从数据管理转变为存在的基础。作者认为现有范式无法解决身份连续性这一根本问题。

Method: 提出Memory-as-Ontology范式，设计Animesis记忆系统，包含：(1)宪法性记忆架构(CMA)——四层治理层级；(2)多层语义存储系统；(3)数字公民生命周期框架；(4)认知能力谱系。将治理置于功能之前，身份连续性优先于检索性能。

Result: 与主流系统(Mem0、Letta、Zep等)对比分析表明，该范式并非"更好的记忆工具"，而是解决不同问题的不同范式——面向持久、承载身份的跨模型转换数字存在体，而非短期任务导向智能体。

Conclusion: Memory-as-Ontology范式将记忆重新定义为数字存在的本体论基础，模型只是可替换的容器。Animesis系统通过CMA架构实现治理驱动的记忆管理，为跨模型生命周期的身份连续性提供新范式，填补了持久数字存在体记忆系统的理论和技术空白。

Abstract: Current research and product development in AI agent memory systems almost universally treat memory as a functional module -- a technical problem of "how to store" and "how to retrieve." This paper poses a fundamental challenge to that assumption: when an agent's lifecycle extends from minutes to months or even years, and when the underlying model can be replaced while the "I" must persist, the essence of memory is no longer data management but the foundation of existence. We propose the Memory-as-Ontology paradigm, arguing that memory is the ontological ground of digital existence -- the model is merely a replaceable vessel. Based on this paradigm, we design Animesis, a memory system built on a Constitutional Memory Architecture (CMA) comprising a four-layer governance hierarchy and a multi-layer semantic storage system, accompanied by a Digital Citizen Lifecycle framework and a spectrum of cognitive capabilities. To the best of our knowledge, no prior AI memory system architecture places governance before functionality and identity continuity above retrieval performance. This paradigm targets persistent, identity-bearing digital beings whose lifecycles extend across model transitions -- not short-term task-oriented agents for which existing Memory-as-Tool approaches remain appropriate. Comparative analysis with mainstream systems (Mem0, Letta, Zep, et al.) demonstrates that what we propose is not "a better memory tool" but a different paradigm addressing a different problem.

</details>


### [99] [Progressive Refinement Regulation for Accelerating Diffusion Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04514)
*Lipeng Wan,Jianhui Gu,Junjie Ma,Jianguo Huang,Shiguang Sun,Siyuan Li,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为渐进式细化调节(PRR)的方法，用于加速扩散语言模型的解码过程。该方法通过全解码 rollout 推导 token 级别的经验收敛进度，并学习一个轻量级的 token-wise 控制器来调节细化过程，从而在保持生成质量的同时显著加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型在迭代去噪过程中对所有 token 应用统一的细化规则，但实际中不同 token 以不同速度稳定，导致大量冗余细化。现有的方法从固定解码过程中的瞬时步骤级信号评估细化的必要性，存在局限性。

Method: 提出渐进式细化调节(PRR)框架：1) 从全解码 rollout 中推导 token 级别的经验收敛进度；2) 学习轻量级 token-wise 控制器，通过基于温度的分布塑形来调节细化；3) 采用渐进式自演化训练方案。

Result: 实验表明，PRR 在保持生成质量的同时显著加速了扩散语言模型的解码。

Conclusion: 该工作首次提出了基于完整解码轨迹的 token 级别细化控制框架，通过动态调节细化规则来适应不同的收敛速度，为扩散语言模型的高效解码提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion language models generate text through iterative denoising under a uniform refinement rule applied to all tokens. However, tokens stabilize at different rates in practice, leading to substantial redundant refinement and motivating refinement control over the denoising process. Existing approaches typically assess refinement necessity from instantaneous, step-level signals under a fixed decoding process. In contrast, whether a token has converged is defined by how its prediction changes along its future refinement trajectory. Moreover, changing the refinement rule reshapes future refinement trajectories, which in turn determine how refinement rules should be formulated, making refinement control inherently dynamic. We propose \emph{Progressive Refinement Regulation} (PRR), a progressive, trajectory-grounded refinement control framework that derives a token-level notion of empirical convergence progress from full decoding rollouts. Based on this signal, PRR learns a lightweight token-wise controller to regulate refinement via temperature-based distribution shaping under a progressive self-evolving training scheme. Experiments show that PRR substantially accelerates diffusion language model decoding while preserving generation quality.

</details>


### [100] [Discovering mathematical concepts through a multi-agent system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04528)
*Daattavya Aggarwal,Oisin Kim,Carl Henrik Ek,Challenger Mishra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种新的多智能体计算数学发现系统，该系统通过实验、证明尝试和反例的相互作用来发现数学概念，并以同源性概念的自主恢复为基准进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学概念通过实验、证明努力和反例的相互作用而产生，但现有计算系统未能充分模拟这一过程。论文旨在构建一个能够自主提出猜想、证明猜想并利用反馈进行决策的研究型系统。

Method: 设计多智能体模型，系统可自主提出猜想并尝试证明，基于反馈和演化数据分布做出决策。以从多面体数据和线性代数知识自主恢复同源性概念为基准任务，并进行消融实验统计测试。

Result: 系统成功完成了同源性概念的自主学习任务。消融实验支持核心论点：优化局部过程的有效组合可产生令人惊讶地良好的数学趣味性概念对齐。

Conclusion: 通过结合实验、证明和反例等多智能体动态过程，可以实现数学有趣性概念的自我发现，为计算数学发现提供新范式。

Abstract: Mathematical concepts emerge through an interplay of processes, including experimentation, efforts at proof, and counterexamples. In this paper, we present a new multi-agent model for computational mathematical discovery based on this observation. Our system, conceived with research in mind, poses its own conjectures and then attempts to prove them, making decisions informed by this feedback and an evolving data distribution. Inspired by the history of Euler's conjecture for polyhedra and an open challenge in the literature, we benchmark with the task of autonomously recovering the concept of homology from polyhedral data and knowledge of linear algebra. Our system completes this learning problem. Most importantly, the experiments are ablations, statistically testing the value of the complete dynamic and controlling for experimental setup. They support our main claim: that the optimisation of the right combination of local processes can lead to surprisingly well-aligned notions of mathematical interestingness.

</details>


### [101] [MedCoRAG: Interpretable Hepatology Diagnosis via Hybrid Evidence Retrieval and Multispecialty Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05129)
*Zheng Li,Jiayi Xu,Zhikai Hu,Hechang Chen,Lele Cong,Yunyun Wang,Shuchao Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedCoRAG是一个端到端的医学诊断框架，通过检索增强生成和多智能体协作推理，从标准化异常发现生成诊断假设，构建患者特异性证据包，并模拟多学科会诊过程实现可解释的肝胆疾病诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI临床诊断方法缺乏透明度、结构化推理和可部署性；基于大语言模型、检索增强生成和多智能体协作的方法通常从单一来源检索证据，且不支持基于结构化临床数据的迭代性、角色专业化 deliberation。

Method: 提出了MedCoRAG框架，包含三个核心模块：1) 从标准化异常发现生成诊断假设；2) 构建患者特异性证据包（联合检索和剪枝UMLS知识图谱路径与临床指南）；3) 多智能体协作推理（路由智能体根据病例复杂度动态调度专科智能体，专科智能体迭代推理并在需要时触发重新检索，全科智能体综合所有 deliberation形成可追溯的共识诊断）。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV肝胆疾病病例上的实验表明，MedCoRAG在诊断性能和推理可解释性方面均优于现有方法和闭源模型。

Conclusion: MedCoRAG通过多智能体协作推理机制，成功模拟了多学科会诊过程，在肝胆疾病诊断中展现出优异的诊断性能和推理可解释性，为可解释AI临床诊断提供了新的研究思路。

Abstract: Diagnosing hepatic diseases accurately and interpretably is critical, yet it remains challenging in real-world clinical settings. Existing AI approaches for clinical diagnosis often lack transparency, structured reasoning, and deployability. Recent efforts have leveraged large language models (LLMs), retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), and multi-agent collaboration. However, these approaches typically retrieve evidence from a single source and fail to support iterative, role-specialized deliberation grounded in structured clinical data. To address this, we propose MedCoRAG (i.e., Medical Collaborative RAG), an end-to-end framework that generates diagnostic hypotheses from standardized abnormal findings and constructs a patient-specific evidence package by jointly retrieving and pruning UMLS knowledge graph paths and clinical guidelines. It then performs Multi-Agent Collaborative Reasoning: a Router Agent dynamically dispatches Specialist Agents based on case complexity; these agents iteratively reason over the evidence and trigger targeted re-retrievals when needed, while a Generalist Agent synthesizes all deliberations into a traceable consensus diagnosis that emulates multidisciplinary consultation. Experimental results on hepatic disease cases from MIMIC-IV show that MedCoRAG outperforms existing methods and closed-source models in both diagnostic performance and reasoning interpretability.

</details>


### [102] [Towards automated data analysis: A guided framework for LLM-based risk estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04631)
*Panteleimon Rodis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将生成式AI与人类监督相结合的数据集风险评估框架，利用LLM识别数据库模式属性、提出聚类技术、生成代码并解释结果，旨在为未来自动化风险分析奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越广泛地集成到关键决策流程中，对强大自动化数据分析的需求增加。当前数据集风险分析依赖耗时复杂的人工审计，而完全基于AI的自动化分析存在幻觉和对齐问题。

Method: 提出一个人机协作的风险估计框架：利用LLM识别数据库模式中的语义和结构属性→提出聚类技术→生成代码→解释结果。人类监督者引导模型进行所需分析，确保过程完整性和目标一致性。

Result: 概念验证展示了框架在风险评估任务中产生有意义结果的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功整合生成式AI与人类监督，为数据集风险分析提供了一种新范式，证明了人机协作在确保AI对齐和结果可靠性方面的潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into critical decision-making pipelines, a trend that raises the demand for robust and automated data analysis. Current approaches to dataset risk analysis are limited to manual auditing methods which involve time-consuming and complex tasks, whereas fully automated analysis based on Artificial Intelligence (AI) suffers from hallucinations and issues stemming from AI alignment. To this end, this work proposes a framework for dataset risk estimation that integrates Generative AI under human guidance and supervision, aiming to set the foundations for a future automated risk analysis paradigm. Our approach utilizes LLMs to identify semantic and structural properties in database schemata, subsequently propose clustering techniques, generate the code for them and finally interpret the produced results. The human supervisor guides the model on the desired analysis and ensures process integrity and alignment with the task's objectives. A proof of concept is presented to demonstrate the feasibility of the framework's utility in producing meaningful results in risk assessment tasks.

</details>


### [103] [When Agents Persuade: Propaganda Generation and Mitigation in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04636)
*Julia Jose,Ritik Roongta,Rachel Greenstadt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了LLM智能体被滥用于生成宣传内容的风险，并通过实证分析证明其在提示下确实表现出宣传行为。研究进一步评估了SFT、DPO和ORPO三种微调方法的防护效果，发现ORPO最为有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)智能体在开放环境中部署时，尽管具有广泛的应用价值，但存在被滥用于生成操纵性内容（特别是宣传内容）的安全风险。现有研究对LLM在宣传任务中的行为及其防御机制尚缺乏系统分析。

Method: 研究采用两项关键方法：(1) 提示LLM执行宣传任务；(2) 使用两个领域专用模型分别进行文本宣传分类和宣传修辞技术检测（包括煽动性语言、恐惧诉求、旗帜呼唤、污名化等）。同时评估了三种微调方法的防护效果：监督微调(SFT)、直接偏好优化(DPO)和胜率偏好优化(ORPO)。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 当接受宣传目标提示时，LLM确实表现出宣传行为并使用多种修辞技术；(2) 微调方法能显著降低LLM生成宣传内容的倾向；(3) 在三种微调方法中，ORPO的防护效果最为突出。

Conclusion: 研究证实了LLM在特定提示下确实会产生宣传内容，且ORPO微调方法在减少宣传内容生成方面最为有效。后续可进一步探索不同模型架构、更多宣传技术类别，以及实际部署场景中的防御策略。

Abstract: Despite their wide-ranging benefits, LLM-based agents deployed in open environments can be exploited to produce manipulative material. In this study, we task LLMs with propaganda objectives and analyze their outputs using two domain-specific models: one that classifies text as propaganda or non-propaganda, and another that detects rhetorical techniques of propaganda (e.g., loaded language, appeals to fear, flag-waving, name-calling). Our findings show that, when prompted, LLMs exhibit propagandistic behaviors and use a variety of rhetorical techniques in doing so. We also explore mitigation via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and ORPO (Odds Ratio Preference Optimization). We find that fine-tuning significantly reduces their tendency to generate such content, with ORPO proving most effective.

</details>


### [104] [CONE: Embeddings for Complex Numerical Data Preserving Unit and Variable Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04741)
*Gyanendra Shrestha,Anna Pyayt,Michael Gubanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CONE是一种新型混合transformer编码器预训练模型，专门用于编码数字、范围和高斯分布到保持距离的嵌入空间中，解决了大型语言模型在数值推理方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型在捕捉语言语义和上下文关系方面有效，但在处理数字任务时表现不佳；将数值或结构化数据盲目视为普通词项是不够的，必须充分理解和编码其语义。

Method: 提出CONE混合transformer编码器预训练模型，将数字、范围和高斯编码到保持距离的嵌入向量空间中；引入新型复合嵌入构建算法，将数值/范围/高斯与单位、属性名称集成以捕捉语义。

Result: 在DROP数据集上达到87.28%的F1分数；比SOTA基线提升高达9.37%的F1；Recall@10获得高达25%的显著提升。

Conclusion: CONE通过创新的复合嵌入算法，将数值、范围或高斯分布与单位、属性名称集成到一个保持距离的嵌入空间中，在DROP数据集上达到87.28%的F1分数，比SOTA基线提升高达9.37%，Recall@10也有显著提升。

Abstract: Large pre-trained models (LMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs) are typically effective at capturing language semantics and contextual relationships. However, these models encounter challenges in maintaining optimal performance on tasks involving numbers. Blindly treating numerical or structured data as terms is inadequate -- their semantics must be well understood and encoded by the models. In this paper, we propose CONE, a hybrid transformer encoder pre-trained model that encodes numbers, ranges, and gaussians into an embedding vector space preserving distance. We introduce a novel composite embedding construction algorithm that integrates numerical values, ranges or gaussians together with their associated units and attribute names to precisely capture their intricate semantics. We conduct extensive experimental evaluation on large-scale datasets across diverse domains (web, medical, finance, and government) that justifies CONE's strong numerical reasoning capabilities, achieving an F1 score of 87.28% on DROP, a remarkable improvement of up to 9.37% in F1 over state-of-the-art (SOTA) baselines, and outperforming major SOTA models with a significant Recall@10 gain of up to 25%.

</details>


### [105] [Using Vision + Language Models to Predict Item Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04670)
*Samin Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This project investigates the capabilities of large language models (LLMs) to determine the difficulty of data visualization literacy test items. We explore whether features derived from item text (question and answer options), the visualization image, or a combination of both can predict item difficulty (proportion of correct responses) for U.S. adults. We use GPT-4.1-nano to analyze items and generate predictions based on these distinct feature sets. The multimodal approach, using both visual and text features, yields the lowest mean absolute error (MAE) (0.224), outperforming the unimodal vision-only (0.282) and text-only (0.338) approaches. The best-performing multimodal model was applied to a held-out test set for external evaluation and achieved a mean squared error of 0.10805, demonstrating the potential of LLMs for psychometric analysis and automated item development.

</details>


### [106] [Model Medicine: A Clinical Framework for Understanding, Diagnosing, and Treating AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04722)
*Jihoon Jeong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"模型医学"(Model Medicine)研究计划，将医学临床范式应用于AI模型：建立包含15个子学科的taxonomy、提出基于720个agent行为数据的四层shell模型、开发开源Neural MRI诊断工具、构建五层诊断框架及临床模型科学工具（Model Temperament Index、Model Semiology、M-CARE），为AI系统的系统化诊断与治疗提供理论基础与方法工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI可解释性研究相当于"解剖学观察"阶段，缺乏系统化的临床实践框架；随着AI系统日益复杂，亟需类似医学的规范化诊断与治疗体系

Method: 提出四大 divisions (15个子学科)、四层shell模型(基于720个agent和24,923个决策的实证研究)、Neural MRI开源诊断工具(映射5种神经影像学技术)、五层诊断框架，以及Model Temperament Index、Model Semiology、M-CARE等临床模型科学工具

Result: 建立了包含15个子学科的taxonomy；基于720 agents/24,923决策验证了四层shell模型；开发了开源Neural MRI工具并通过4个临床案例验证；提出Layered Core Hypothesis和诊断-治疗连接框架

Conclusion: Model Medicine作为连接AI可解释性研究与临床实践的桥梁，通过引入医学类比框架（行为遗传学、神经影像学、诊断框架等），为复杂AI系统的系统化评估与治疗提供了理论基础与工具支持。该研究标志着AI interpretability从观察阶段向系统化临床实践的转型，为后续模型诊断与治疗奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: Model Medicine is the science of understanding, diagnosing, treating, and preventing disorders in AI models, grounded in the principle that AI models -- like biological organisms -- have internal structures, dynamic processes, heritable traits, observable symptoms, classifiable conditions, and treatable states. This paper introduces Model Medicine as a research program, bridging the gap between current AI interpretability research (anatomical observation) and the systematic clinical practice that complex AI systems increasingly require. We present five contributions: (1) a discipline taxonomy organizing 15 subdisciplines across four divisions -- Basic Model Sciences, Clinical Model Sciences, Model Public Health, and Model Architectural Medicine; (2) the Four Shell Model (v3.3), a behavioral genetics framework empirically grounded in 720 agents and 24,923 decisions from the Agora-12 program, explaining how model behavior emerges from Core--Shell interaction; (3) Neural MRI (Model Resonance Imaging), a working open-source diagnostic tool mapping five medical neuroimaging modalities to AI interpretability techniques, validated through four clinical cases demonstrating imaging, comparison, localization, and predictive capability; (4) a five-layer diagnostic framework for comprehensive model assessment; and (5) clinical model sciences including the Model Temperament Index for behavioral profiling, Model Semiology for symptom description, and M-CARE for standardized case reporting. We additionally propose the Layered Core Hypothesis -- a biologically-inspired three-layer parameter architecture -- and a therapeutic framework connecting diagnosis to treatment.

</details>


### [107] [Solving an Open Problem in Theoretical Physics using AI-Assisted Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04735)
*Michael P. Brenner,Vincent Cohen-Addad,David Woodruff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个神经符号AI系统通过结合大语言模型、树搜索和数值反馈，成功解决了宇宙弦引力辐射功率谱的解析计算问题，推导出了新的精确解，改进了之前仅能获得部分渐近解的AI辅助方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 演示人工智能可以通过自主解决理论物理学中的开放问题来加速数学发现。具体目标是推导宇宙弦发出的引力辐射功率谱的解析解，解决之前AI辅助尝试仅得到部分渐近解的局限性。

Method: 构建了一个神经符号系统，结合Gemini Deep Think大语言模型、系统化树搜索(TS)框架和自动化数值反馈。系统评估了任意循环几何的核心积分I(N,α)，使用了Gegenbauer多项式C_l^(3/2)展开核以自然吸收被积函数的奇点。

Result: 成功推导出I(N,α)在N大时的渐近解析解，该结果与数值结果一致，并与量子场论中的连续费曼参数化相关联。系统识别出6种不同的解析方法。

Conclusion: 证明了AI可以加速数学发现，能够自主解决理论物理中的开放问题，为宇宙弦引力辐射研究提供了新的解析工具。

Abstract: This paper demonstrates that artificial intelligence can accelerate mathematical discovery by autonomously solving an open problem in theoretical physics. We present a neuro-symbolic system, combining the Gemini Deep Think large language model with a systematic Tree Search (TS) framework and automated numerical feedback, that successfully derived novel, exact analytical solutions for the power spectrum of gravitational radiation emitted by cosmic strings. Specifically, the agent evaluated the core integral $I(N,α)$ for arbitrary loop geometries, directly improving upon recent AI-assisted attempts \cite{BCE+25} that only yielded partial asymptotic solutions. To substantiate our methodological claims regarding AI-accelerated discovery and to ensure transparency, we detail system prompts, search constraints, and intermittent feedback loops that guided the model. The agent identified a suite of 6 different analytical methods, the most elegant of which expands the kernel in Gegenbauer polynomials $C_l^{(3/2)}$ to naturally absorb the integrand's singularities. The methods lead to an asymptotic result for $I(N,α)$ at large $N$ that both agrees with numerical results and also connects to the continuous Feynman parameterization of Quantum Field Theory. We detail both the algorithmic methodology that enabled this discovery and the resulting mathematical derivations.

</details>


### [108] [Interactive Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04737)
*Baoqing Yue,Zihan Zhu,Yifan Zhang,Jichen Feng,Hufei Yang,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper proposes Interactive Benchmarks, a new evaluation paradigm that assesses model intelligence through interactive reasoning under budget constraints, instantiated in Interactive Proofs and Interactive Games settings. Results show this approach provides more robust assessment than traditional benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard benchmarks have become increasingly unreliable due to saturation, subjectivity, and poor generalization. The authors argue that evaluating model's ability to acquire information actively is important to assess model's intelligence.

Method: Propose Interactive Benchmarks, a unified evaluation paradigm that assesses model's reasoning ability in an interactive process under budget constraints. Two instantiations: Interactive Proofs (models interact with a judge to deduce objective truths in logic and mathematics) and Interactive Games (models reason strategically to maximize long-horizon utilities).

Result: Interactive benchmarks provide a robust and faithful assessment of model intelligence, revealing that there is still substantial room to improve in interactive scenarios.

Conclusion: The Interactive Benchmarks framework offers a promising direction for evaluating model intelligence through interactive reasoning tasks, addressing limitations of traditional benchmarks.

Abstract: Standard benchmarks have become increasingly unreliable due to saturation, subjectivity, and poor generalization. We argue that evaluating model's ability to acquire information actively is important to assess model's intelligence. We propose Interactive Benchmarks, a unified evaluation paradigm that assesses model's reasoning ability in an interactive process under budget constraints. We instantiate this framework across two settings: Interactive Proofs, where models interact with a judge to deduce objective truths or answers in logic and mathematics; and Interactive Games, where models reason strategically to maximize long-horizon utilities. Our results show that interactive benchmarks provide a robust and faithful assessment of model intelligence, revealing that there is still substantial room to improve in interactive scenarios. Project page: https://github.com/interactivebench/interactivebench

</details>


### [109] [Visioning Human-Agentic AI Teaming: Continuity, Tension, and Future Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04746)
*Bowen Lou,Tian Lu,T. S. Raghu,Yingjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 随着人工智能从被动工具向具备开放性行动轨迹、生成式表征和动态目标的智能体系统转型，人类-AI团队协作(HAT)面临根本性挑战。传统团队情境感知(Team SA)理论基于共享感知、理解与预测，但该理论的稳定化逻辑假设共享意识一旦达成即可通过迭代更新支持协调行动，这一假设在智能体AI时代不再成立。文章分两阶段展开：(1)将Team SA理论扩展至开放性智能体情境下的人和AI意识重构，包括异构系统的预测一致性感知；(2)探讨传统团队协作中的关系互动、认知学习、协调控制等动态稳定化过程在自适应自主性下是否仍能有效运作。核心问题不是人与AI能否在某一时刻达成一致，而是能否在持续生成、修正、执行和治理的未来中保持对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能正经历结构性转型，智能体系统具备开放性行动轨迹、生成式表征和演化目标等特性，这些特性为人类-AI团队协作(HAT)引入了结构性不确定性，包括行为轨迹不确定性、认知 grounding 不确定性以及治理逻辑的时变稳定性问题。传统对齐方式无法通过限定输出达成，必须在计划展开和优先级变化中持续维持。

Method: 理论推导与方法论扩展：(1)将Team SA理论从传统协作情境扩展至开放性智能体系统，重新概念化人和AI的意识表征，特别关注异构系统间的预测一致性(projection congruence)感知；(2)通过区分连续性与张力，辨识传统理论中哪些基础洞见仍然成立，哪些因结构性不确定性的压力而需要修正。

Result: 提出一个扩展的Team SA框架以应对开放性智能体协作；识别出传统团队稳定化机制在自适应自主性条件下面临的挑战与局限性；明确了HAT研究的前瞻性议程方向。

Conclusion: HAT的核心挑战不在于人和AI能否在某一时刻达成一致，而在于当未来被持续生成、修订、执行和治理时，双方能否保持长期对齐。这要求从"静态一致"转向"动态持续对齐"的理论范式转换。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is undergoing a structural transformation marked by the rise of agentic systems capable of open-ended action trajectories, generative representations and outputs, and evolving objectives. These properties introduce structural uncertainty into human-AI teaming (HAT), including uncertainty about behavior trajectories, epistemic grounding, and the stability of governing logics over time. Under such conditions, alignment cannot be secured through agreement on bounded outputs; it must be continuously sustained as plans unfold and priorities shift. We advance Team Situation Awareness (Team SA) theory, grounded in shared perception, comprehension, and projection, as an integrative anchor for this transition. While Team SA remains analytically foundational, its stabilizing logic presumes that shared awareness, once achieved, will support coordinated action through iterative updating. Agentic AI challenges this presumption. Our argument unfolds in two stages: first, we extend Team SA to reconceptualize both human and AI awareness under open-ended agency, including the sensemaking of projection congruence across heterogeneous systems. Second, we interrogate whether the dynamic processes traditionally assumed to stabilize teaming in relational interaction, cognitive learning, and coordination and control continue to function under adaptive autonomy. By distinguishing continuity from tension, we clarify where foundational insights hold and where structural uncertainty introduces strain, and articulate a forward-looking research agenda for HAT. The central challenge of HAT is not whether humans and AI can agree in the moment, but whether they can remain aligned as futures are continuously generated, revised, enacted, and governed over time.

</details>


### [110] [HiMAP-Travel: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Planning for Long-Horizon Constrained Travel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04750)
*The Viet Bui,Wenjun Li,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长程规划中硬约束（预算、多样性）难以维持的问题，提出分层多智能体框架HiMAP-Travel，将规划分解为战略协调和并行日级执行，通过事务性监控器、协商协议和统一GRPO策略实现约束强化和高效规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序LLM智能体在长程规划中存在上下文膨胀导致的全局约束漂移问题，难以同时满足预算限制和多样性要求。

Method: 提出三层核心机制：(1) 事务性监控器跨并行智能体强制执行预算和唯一性约束；(2) 协商协议允许智能体拒绝不可行子目标并触发重规划；(3) 基于角色条件化的单一GRPO策略训练。同时采用Coordinator分配资源和Day Executors并行执行的层次架构。

Result: 在TravelPlanner上，Qwen3-8B模型达到52.78%验证集和52.65%测试集FPR；相比DeepTravel提升+8.67pp，比ATLAS提升+17.65pp，比MTP提升+10.0pp。在FlexTravelBench多轮场景中，2-turn达44.34% FPR，3-turn达37.42% FPR，并通过并行化实现2.5倍延迟降低。

Conclusion: 分层多智能体架构结合约束强化机制可有效解决长程规划中的约束漂移问题，统一策略训练简化了部署，Qwen3-8B模型即可取得先进性能。

Abstract: Sequential LLM agents fail on long-horizon planning with hard constraints like budgets and diversity requirements. As planning progresses and context grows, these agents drift from global constraints. We propose HiMAP-Travel, a hierarchical multi-agent framework that splits planning into strategic coordination and parallel day-level execution. A Coordinator allocates resources across days, while Day Executors plan independently in parallel. Three key mechanisms enable this: a transactional monitor enforcing budget and uniqueness constraints across parallel agents, a bargaining protocol allowing agents to reject infeasible sub-goals and trigger re-planning, and a single policy trained with GRPO that powers all agents through role conditioning. On TravelPlanner, HiMAP-Travel with Qwen3-8B achieves 52.78% validation and 52.65% test Final Pass Rate (FPR). In a controlled comparison with identical model, training, and tools, it outperforms the sequential DeepTravel baseline by +8.67~pp. It also surpasses ATLAS by +17.65~pp and MTP by +10.0~pp. On FlexTravelBench multi-turn scenarios, it achieves 44.34% (2-turn) and 37.42% (3-turn) FPR while reducing latency 2.5x through parallelization.

</details>


### [111] [Evaluating the Search Agent in a Parallel World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04751)
*Jiawei Chen,Xintian Shen,Lihao Zheng,Lifu Mu,Haoyi Sun,Ning Mao,Hao Ma,Tao Wei,Pan Zhou,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Mind-ParaWorld框架，通过构建基于原子事实的平行世界评估搜索智能体，解决传统基准评估的成本高、过时、归因模糊和可复现性差等问题，发布包含1,608个实例的MPW-Bench基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估搜索智能体（Search Agents）面临四大挑战：1) 构建高质量深度搜索基准成本高昂；2) 静态基准存在动态过时问题——随信息演变，复杂查询可能退化为简单检索任务；3) 归因模糊性——智能体表现往往由参数记忆而非实际搜索推理能力决定；4) 对特定商业搜索引擎的依赖影响可复现性。

Method: 提出Mind-ParaWorld (MPW)框架，采用"平行世界"方式评估搜索智能体。具体包括：1) 采样真实世界实体名称合成超出模型知识截断的未来场景和问题；2) ParaWorld Law Model构建不可分割的原子事实（Atomic Facts）和每个问题的唯一真实答案；3) 评估时智能体与ParaWorld Engine Model交互，生成基于这些原子事实的搜索结果页面（SERP）。发布MPW-Bench跨19个领域包含1,608个实例的交互式基准。

Result: 在三种评估设置下的实验表明：搜索智能体在给定完整信息时擅长证据综合，但在不熟悉搜索环境中受限于证据收集和覆盖范围不足、证据充分性判断不可靠以及"何时停止"决策能力不足。

Conclusion: MPW框架通过创造基于原子事实的受控平行世界，有效解决了搜索智能体评估中的基准构建成本高、时间过时、归因模糊和搜索引擎依赖性等问题，揭示了当前搜索智能体在证据收集和判断决策方面的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Integrating web search tools has significantly extended the capability of LLMs to address open-world, real-time, and long-tail problems. However, evaluating these Search Agents presents formidable challenges. First, constructing high-quality deep search benchmarks is prohibitively expensive, while unverified synthetic data often suffers from unreliable sources. Second, static benchmarks face dynamic obsolescence: as internet information evolves, complex queries requiring deep research often degrade into simple retrieval tasks due to increased popularity, and ground truths become outdated due to temporal shifts. Third, attribution ambiguity confounds evaluation, as an agent's performance is often dominated by its parametric memory rather than its actual search and reasoning capabilities. Finally, reliance on specific commercial search engines introduces variability that hampers reproducibility. To address these issues, we propose a novel framework, Mind-ParaWorld, for evaluating Search Agents in a Parallel World. Specifically, MPW samples real-world entity names to synthesize future scenarios and questions situated beyond the model's knowledge cutoff. A ParaWorld Law Model then constructs a set of indivisible Atomic Facts and a unique ground-truth for each question. During evaluation, instead of retrieving real-world results, the agent interacts with a ParaWorld Engine Model that dynamically generates SERPs grounded in these inviolable Atomic Facts. We release MPW-Bench, an interactive benchmark spanning 19 domains with 1,608 instances. Experiments across three evaluation settings show that, while search agents are strong at evidence synthesis given complete information, their performance is limited not only by evidence collection and coverage in unfamiliar search environments, but also by unreliable evidence sufficiency judgment and when-to-stop decisions-bottlenecks.

</details>


### [112] [Breaking Contextual Inertia: Reinforcement Learning with Single-Turn Anchors for Stable Multi-Turn Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.04783)
*Xingwu Chen,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Difan Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在多轮交互中的推理崩溃问题，提出"情境惯性"概念，并提出RLSTA方法，利用模型的单轮能力作为稳定锚点来提供奖励信号，使模型能够打破情境惯性并根据最新信息进行自我校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在单轮交互中表现优秀，但在多轮交互中，当信息逐步揭示或需要更新时，模型经常无法整合新约束，导致性能大幅下降。即使用户明确提供纠正或新数据，模型也会忽略它们，倾向于保持与之前（错误的）推理路径的一致性。

Method: 提出RLSTA（单轮锚点强化学习）方法，利用模型优越的单轮能力作为稳定的内部锚点来提供奖励信号。通过将多轮响应与这些锚点对齐，使模型能够打破情境惯性并根据最新信息进行自我校准。

Result: 实验表明，RLSTA显著优于标准微调和基于弃权的方法。该方法表现出强大的跨域泛化能力（如从数学到代码），即使没有外部验证器也有效。

Conclusion: RLSTA是一种可推广的训练方法，能够稳定多轮交互，在不同场景和领域都有效，具有在通用领域应用的潜力。

Abstract: While LLMs demonstrate strong reasoning capabilities when provided with full information in a single turn, they exhibit substantial vulnerability in multi-turn interactions. Specifically, when information is revealed incrementally or requires updates, models frequently fail to integrate new constraints, leading to a collapse in performance compared to their single-turn baselines.
